+function macosStateFile({create=false}={}){
+  const root=path.resolve(stateRoot({create:false})),home=path.resolve(os.homedir());
+  if(!root.startsWith(home+path.sep)) throw new Error("macOS installer state must be isolated inside canonical HOME");
+  let current=path.parse(root).root;

+    current=path.join(current,part);

+    const within=current===home||current.startsWith(home+path.sep);

+function loadState(options={}){
+  if(process.platform==="darwin"){
+    const empty=()=>({schema_version:1,preferred_language:null,current_step:"not_started",completed:[],ready:false});

+    try{file=macosStateFile();}catch(error){if(error.code!=="ENOENT")throw error;return empty();}
+    let fd;
+    try{
+      const before=fs.lstatSync(file);
+      fd=fs.openSync(file,fs.constants.O_RDONLY|(fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW||0));
+      const opened=fs.fstatSync(fd);
+      if(!opened.isFile()||opened.nlink!==1||opened.ino!==before.ino||opened.dev!==before.dev||(opened.mode&0o777)!==0o600||(typeof process.getuid==="function"&&opened.uid!==process.getuid())) throw new Error("macOS installer state changed during read");
+      const state=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(fd,"utf8"));

+      return state;
+    }catch(error){if(error.code==="ENOENT")return empty();throw error;}
+    finally{if(fd!==undefined)fs.closeSync(fd);}
+  }
+  return readInstallerState(options);
+}

+function saveState(state){

+  const file=macosStateFile({create:true});
+  const temporary=`${file}.${process.pid}.${crypto.randomUUID()}.tmp`;
+  const fd=fs.openSync(temporary,"wx",0o600);
+  try{fs.writeFileSync(fd,JSON.stringify(state,null,2)+"\n");fs.fsyncSync(fd);}finally{fs.closeSync(fd);}
+  macosStateFile();
+  fs.renameSync(temporary,file);
+  const directory=fs.openSync(path.dirname(file),"r");

 function emit(payload, code = 0) {
   if (wantsJson) process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2) + "\n");
   else {
     const zh=payload.preferred_language==="zh-CN";
     console.log(payload.message || payload.status || "");

   const report=sanitizeObject({generated_at:new Date().toISOString(),version:productConfig.version||"unknown",release_channel:productConfig.release_channel||null,platform:process.platform,os_release:os.release(),current_step:state.current_step||null,preferred_language:lang,last_error:state.last_error||null,environment:state.environment||null});
   const dir=path.join(stateRoot(),"diagnostics");
   fs.mkdirSync(dir,{recursive:true});
   const file=path.join(dir,`diagnostic-${Date.now()}.json`);
   fs.writeFileSync(file,JSON.stringify(report,null,2));
   emit({status:"PASS",preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"report.prepared"),report_file:file,preview:report,uploaded:false,consent_required_before_upload:true});
 }
 
 function submitReport() {
   const state=loadState();
   const lang=ensureStateLanguage(state);
   const file=valueOf("--file");
   const consent=/^(true|yes|y|1)$/i.test(String(valueOf("--consent")||""));
   const feedbackRepository=process.env.ORCHESTRATOR_FEEDBACK_REPOSITORY||productConfig.feedback_repository||"";
   if(!consent){
     return emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"REPORT-001",recoverable:false,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"report.consent.required"),uploaded:false},1);
   }
   if(!file||!fs.existsSync(file)){
     return emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"REPORT-002",recoverable:true,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"report.file.missing"),uploaded:false},1);
   }
   if(!/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$/.test(feedbackRepository)){
     return emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"REPORT-003",recoverable:true,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"report.destination.missing"),uploaded:false},1);
   }
   try{
     const result=submitDiagnosticReport({file,repository:feedbackRepository});
     emit({status:"PASS",preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"report.submitted"),uploaded:true,url:result.url||null});
   }catch(error){
     emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"REPORT-004",recoverable:true,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"report.submit.failed"),details:String(error.message||error),uploaded:false},1);
   }
 }
 
 if (command==="dry-run") dryRun();
 else if (command==="setup"||command==="resume") runSetup();

 Windows interactive bootstrap:
 
 `Install.cmd`
 
 macOS official bootstrap:
 
 `Install.command` (or `./install.sh`)
 
 The macOS entrypoint performs a read-only prerequisite and migration preflight first. It does not install services, create repositories, activate a runner, copy credentials, or change an existing runner. macOS setup remains approval-gated and uses the same CLI state/resume path.
 
 macOS recovery also uses the versioned Trusted Recovery Evidence Contract v1. It independently cross-checks the exact private Control and Sandbox repository identities and immutable GitHub repository IDs, the authored explicit Chat registration issue, and securely owned local instance/migration/receipt metadata. Missing or legacy proof is `NEEDS_HUMAN`; it is never inferred from installer state, a local checkout, a browser profile, or a default Chat route. First boot may remain `ABSENT` until the generated repositories and explicit route exist; the receipt is issued only after the existing approval, authorization, and route gates pass. Historical migration is a separate human-approved procedure and is not performed by recovery preflight.
 
 On macOS, the preflight also reports the reviewed local libproc helper capability. The helper is built only after the existing macOS setup approval and exact installation/private-instance checks. Dry-run, doctor, status, and an unapproved setup do not compile or write it. A missing Apple Command Line Tools/SDK, source-digest mismatch, unsafe private path, failed PID proof, interrupted build, or receipt/binary tamper remains `NEEDS_USER_ACTION` or `ERROR`; it never enables native identity or silently claims the legacy mode is native.
 
-The locally built helper is unsigned and is distinct from any separately gated signing, distribution, or release process. This iteration does not enable Runner, launchctl, production environment variables, target activation, or live installation. Manual receipt/lock recovery must preserve the receipt, establish that no writer remains, and use the reviewed deployment recovery procedure; an uncertain lock must not be deleted as if it belonged to another writer. The full isolated live Sandbox E2E remains separately gated.
+The locally built helper is unsigned and is distinct from any separately gated signing, distribution, or release process. Runner installation and start require the additional first-boot approvals below. Manual receipt/lock recovery must preserve the receipt, establish that no writer remains, and use the reviewed deployment recovery procedure; an uncertain lock must not be deleted as if it belonged to another writer. The full isolated live Sandbox E2E remains separately gated.
+
+## V2.1 M1/M2 macOS ARM64 first boot
+
+The architecture remains GitHub Control -> one self-hosted macOS ARM64 Runner -> local authenticated Codex -> independent wrapper -> the exact registered Chat callback. There is no per-task dispatcher. An ad hoc bootstrap route such as `chat-408` is not installed Runner authority. Preserve historical #404–#406 and closed manual #409; do not rerun them or reopen #409. A new conversation registers separately.
+
+Default recovery still inspects all instances and refuses unsafe or ambiguous historical evidence. It never repairs permissions, adopts, migrates, deletes, or writes an old instance. The explicit alternative is `dry-run --selected-fresh-instance <exact-instance-id> --json`, with already prepared installer state naming that same fresh instance. Only this selected path can regard historical roots as nonauthority. It still checks private ownership, every ancestor, symlinks, hardlinks, identity/name/ID collisions, exact generated roles, and independently queried authenticated GitHub owner and distinct immutable private Control/Sandbox IDs.
+
+If that read-only inspection returns `macos_selected_fresh_instance_approval`, present the exact `approval_challenge`. Only a separate human answer authorizes `answer --action macos_selected_fresh_instance_approval --selected-fresh-instance <exact-instance-id> --value '<exact returned challenge>' --json`. The installer creates an exclusive durable receipt inside the selected instance and verifies it again. General setup approval, a route, an old receipt, or a boolean cannot substitute for this approval. A completely empty candidate or conflicting evidence remains blocked; this adapter cannot adopt it into authority.
+
+Official setup/resume then advances in this order:
+
+1. Verify exact installation/owner identity and setup approval; prepare only a new private Mac Control using `orchestrator.macos.yml.template` and its isolated Sandbox. The existing Windows Control and its workflow are preserved. The target registry entry stays disabled.
+2. Obtain the separate ChatGPT GitHub connector authorization for the exact generated private pair and originating Chat URL. Independently requery immutable repository/owner IDs. Local `gh` login alone does not prove connector access.
+3. Prepare and verify the reviewed native helper capability, including its existing native PID identity checks. Require Sandbox folder trust and login to the isolated ChatGPT browser profile. Retain the existing 500ms native identity contract; do not replace it with a fabricated process receipt.
+4. Stop at `provision_macos_runner` unless a separately approved official `actions/runner` macOS ARM64 release has actually been acquired and registered for this generated Control. This implementation does **not** download, extract, configure, register, or start a Runner as part of provisioning. An independently reviewed provisioning procedure must supply the retained official archive and private candidate descriptor; do not ask a user to hand-edit JSON, invent IDs, or reuse a production Runner. Until that procedure and acquisition/registration approval exist, report **PARTIAL / BLOCKED at provisioning**, and leave services and registration untouched.
+5. Inspect the candidate's exact release URL/version and immutable release/asset IDs against the official GitHub release digest, retained archive and complete binary manifest; a release without an authoritative digest remains blocked. Verify the native ARM64 executable, private `.runner`/credential files, config digest, real Runner ID, generated repository immutable IDs, one unique runner/label set, isolated loopback browser profile/port/outbox and authentic ChatGPT Codex login. The candidate pins `runtime_path` to `/opt/homebrew/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`; trusted Node and GitHub CLI must resolve there, with Node matching the installer. The launch path is the approved `instance/runner/bin/Runner.Listener` executable with `run`, never the runner directory. A missing executable, wrong OS/architecture/label, extra runner, foreign owner, unsafe mode/link, or contradictory receipt fails closed.
+6. Present `macos_runner_package_approval` with its exact candidate and returned digest challenge. After explicit human approval, `answer --action macos_runner_package_approval --value '<exact challenge>' --json` exclusively persists the checked approval; it does not acquire, register, install, or start anything.
+7. Present `macos_runner_install` and then `macos_runner_start` as two separate required human actions. Each uses `answer --action <returned action_id> --value '<exact approval_challenge>' --json`, bound to the current installation, repositories, Runner and package. Resume exclusively claims the one-time action, consumes that approval and durably records intent before each operation. Claims remain preserved; a concurrent resume cannot reclaim one. Installing the LaunchAgent does not start it. Starting requires live native capability and the exact persisted plist. An interrupted or uncertain action preserves its journal and requires independent manual recovery; it cannot automatically repeat or overwrite a plist.
+8. Requery the real GitHub Runner and exact loaded LaunchAgent. Publish the first `[CHAT-REGISTER]` only after the one matching macOS ARM64 Runner is online. Registration itself needs no preexisting Chat route. The Mac workflow uses **only `register-chat.mjs --deliver`** as registration delivery owner, pins the supplied Chat URL, serializes `callback_id`, bounds its child process, and retains durable error evidence. After a send attempt or timeout, retries reconcile the same Chat for a real user-role receipt and cannot submit again. A queued draft or invented terminal receipt is not success. There is no fallback Chat or second outbox delivery owner.
+
+Runner package approval files and native capability receipts are different evidence with different authority. Never manufacture either to satisfy a gate. The adapter supplies no approval to execute any of these steps during development. The independent wrapper must perform validation separately; an implementation task that forbids validation must not run tests, build, lint, install dependencies, invoke setup/resume, open a browser/CDP session, deliver callbacks, or install/start services.
+
+The existing macOS post-Sandbox Code Review/merge and target-activation refusal remains in place for M6. Verified Sandbox completion does not authorize main/dev merges, production deployment, or target activation in M1/M2.
 
 AI / structured bootstrap:
 
 `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\install.ps1 -Json -Language <en|zh-CN>`
 
 After Node bootstrap:
 
 - dry-run: `node src/cli.mjs dry-run --json --language <en|zh-CN>`
 - setup: `node src/cli.mjs setup --json --language <en|zh-CN>`
 - save a verified user answer: `node src/cli.mjs answer --action <action_id> --value <value> --json`
 - resume: `node src/cli.mjs resume --json`
 - refresh runtime: `node src/cli.mjs refresh-runtime --json`
 - doctor: `node src/cli.mjs doctor --json`
 - status: `node src/cli.mjs status --json`
 - repair: `node src/cli.mjs repair --json`

 export function renderWorkflow(template,{runnerLabel,platform=process.platform,arch,trustedGithubUsername}={}){
   if(!/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,64}$/.test(runnerLabel)) throw new Error("Invalid runner label");
   const runnerArch=arch==="arm64"?"ARM64":arch==="x64"?"X64":null;
   if(String(template).includes("{{RUNNER_ARCH}}")&&!runnerArch) throw new Error("Invalid runner architecture");
   const rendered=String(template).replaceAll("{{RUNNER_LABEL}}",runnerLabel).replaceAll("{{RUNNER_OS}}",platform==="darwin"?"macOS":"Windows").replaceAll("{{RUNNER_ARCH}}",runnerArch||"");
   if(rendered.includes("{{TRUSTED_GITHUB_USERNAME}}")&&!/^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$/.test(String(trustedGithubUsername||""))) throw new Error("Invalid trusted GitHub login");
   return rendered.replaceAll("{{TRUSTED_GITHUB_USERNAME}}",String(trustedGithubUsername||""));
 }

 }

 name: ChatGPT Codex Orchestrator (macOS)
 on:
   issues:
     types: [opened]
 concurrency:
   group: chatgpt-codex-orchestrator
   queue: max
 permissions:
   contents: read
   issues: read
 jobs:
   register-chat:
     if: startsWith(github.event.issue.title, '[CHAT-REGISTER]')
+    timeout-minutes: 5
     runs-on: [self-hosted, macOS, {{RUNNER_ARCH}}, {{RUNNER_LABEL}}]
     steps:
       - uses: actions/checkout@v4
       - name: Register explicit Chat callback route
         env:
           CODEX_EVENT_PATH: ${{ github.event_path }}
-        run: node scripts/register-chat.mjs --event-path "$CODEX_EVENT_PATH" --control-root "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE" --trusted-github-username "{{TRUSTED_GITHUB_USERNAME}}"
+        run: node scripts/register-chat.mjs --event-path "$CODEX_EVENT_PATH" --control-root "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE" --trusted-github-username "{{TRUSTED_GITHUB_USERNAME}}" --deliver
   run-codex:
     if: startsWith(github.event.issue.title, '[CODEX-RUN]')
     runs-on: [self-hosted, macOS, {{RUNNER_ARCH}}, {{RUNNER_LABEL}}]
     steps:
       - uses: actions/checkout@v4
       - name: Execute native Codex run and finalize
         env:
           CODEX_EVENT_PATH: ${{ github.event_path }}
         run: node scripts/macos-dispatch.mjs --event-path "$CODEX_EVENT_PATH" --control-root "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE" --control-repository "${{ github.repository }}" --trusted-github-username "{{TRUSTED_GITHUB_USERNAME}}"
   code-review:
     if: startsWith(github.event.issue.title, '[CODE-REVIEW]')
     permissions:
       contents: write
       issues: read
     runs-on: [self-hosted, macOS, {{RUNNER_ARCH}}, {{RUNNER_LABEL}}]

+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import fs from "node:fs";
+import os from "node:os";
+import path from "node:path";
+import crypto from "node:crypto";
+import vm from "node:vm";
+import {canonicalInstallationId} from "../src/lib/control-render.mjs";
+
+const cli=fs.readFileSync(new URL("../src/cli.mjs",import.meta.url),"utf8");
+const helpers=cli.slice(cli.indexOf("function macosRunnerActionChallenge("),cli.indexOf("function dryRun()"));
+
+function fixture(t){
+  const home=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir()),"macos-firstboot-synthetic-"));
+  t.after(()=>fs.rmSync(home,{recursive:true,force:true}));
+  const state={installation_id:"abcd1234-test",instance_id:"abcd1234",instance_root:path.join(home,".chatgpt-codex-orchestrator","instances","abcd1234"),control_repository:"owner/chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-control-abcd1234",control_repository_id:"101",sandbox_repository:"owner/chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-sandbox-abcd1234",sandbox_repository_id:"202",sandbox_project_key:"sandbox-abcd1234",runner_label:"codex-orchestrator-abcd1234",browser_profile:path.join(home,"profile"),browser_port:19432,pending_wake_outbox:path.join(home,"outbox")};
+  const runner={status:"PASS",runner_id:"303",runner_executable:path.join(state.instance_root,"runner","bin","Runner.Listener"),approval_sha256:"a".repeat(64)};
+  const calls=[],snapshots=[];
+  const settings={online:false,fail:null,plist:true};
+  const context=vm.createContext({crypto,os,fs,path,process,canonicalInstallationId,inspectMacosRunnerBootstrap:()=>{throw Error("No real inspector in synthetic test");},macosRuntimeAction:()=>{throw Error("No real service in synthetic test");},saveState:()=>{throw Error("No real installer state in synthetic test");}});
+  vm.runInContext(helpers+"\nthis.advance=advanceMacosFirstBootRunner;this.challenge=macosRunnerActionChallenge;this.claim=claimMacosFirstBootOperation;",context);
+  const inspect=({requireOnline})=>{calls.push(requireOnline?"inspect-online":"inspect-package");return requireOnline&&!settings.online?{status:"NEEDS_USER_ACTION",error_id:"MACOS-RUNNER-OFFLINE"}:{...runner};};
+  const runtime=(operation,{state:actual})=>{
+    calls.push(operation);
+    if(operation!=="status"){
+      assert.equal(actual.macos_action_approval,operation);
+      assert.equal(state.macos_runner_action_approval,undefined,"approval consumed before service operation");
+      assert.equal(snapshots.at(-1).macos_runner_bootstrap.phase,operation==="install"?"INSTALL_INTENT":"START_INTENT");
+    }
+    if(settings.fail===operation) return {status:"ERROR",error_id:"SYNTHETIC-INTERRUPTION",mutations_performed:"UNKNOWN"};
+    return {status:"PASS",launchctl_state:"loaded",plist_verified:settings.plist,mutations_performed:operation!=="status"};
+  };
+  return {home,state,runner,calls,snapshots,settings,claim:action=>context.claim({state,action,challenge:context.challenge(action,state,runner),home}),challenge:op=>context.challenge(op,state,runner),advance:()=>context.advance(state,{home,inspect,runtime,claim:()=>({status:"OWNED"}),persist:state=>snapshots.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state)))})};
+}
+
+test("first boot separately approves install/start then waits for a real matching online Runner",t=>{
+  const f=fixture(t);
+  let result=f.advance();
+  assert.equal(result.action_id,"macos_runner_install");
+  assert.equal(f.calls.includes("install"),false);
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=result.approval_challenge;
+  result=f.advance();
+  assert.equal(result.action_id,"macos_runner_start");
+  assert.equal(f.state.macos_runner_bootstrap.phase,"PLIST_INSTALLED");
+  assert.equal(f.calls.includes("start"),false);
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=result.approval_challenge;
+  result=f.advance();
+  assert.equal(result.status,"WAITING");
+  assert.equal(result.action_id,"macos_runner_online");
+  assert.equal(f.state.macos_runner_bootstrap.phase,"STARTED");
+  f.settings.online=true;
+  result=f.advance();
+  assert.equal(result.status,"READY");
+  assert.equal(f.calls.filter(x=>x==="install").length,1);
+  assert.equal(f.calls.filter(x=>x==="start").length,1);
+  assert.equal(f.state.sandbox_review_route,undefined,"registration does not need its own preexisting Chat route");
+});
+
+test("ambiguous service action preserves its journal and cannot automatically repeat",t=>{
+  const f=fixture(t);
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=f.challenge("install");
+  f.settings.fail="install";
+  assert.equal(f.advance().recovery_status,"MANUAL_RECOVERY_REQUIRED");
+  assert.equal(f.state.macos_runner_bootstrap.phase,"ERROR");
+  f.settings.fail=null;
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=f.challenge("install");
+  assert.equal(f.advance().error_id,"MACOS-FIRST-BOOT-INTERRUPTED");
+  assert.equal(f.calls.filter(x=>x==="install").length,1);
+});
+
+test("one-time operation claims cannot be shared or reclaimed by a second resume",t=>{
+  const f=fixture(t);
+  fs.mkdirSync(f.state.instance_root,{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
+  const first=f.claim("install");
+  assert.equal(first.status,"OWNED");
+  const bytes=fs.readFileSync(first.path);
+  assert.equal(f.claim("install").error_id,"MACOS-FIRST-BOOT-ALREADY-CLAIMED");
+  assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(first.path),bytes);
+  assert.equal(f.claim("start").status,"OWNED","start has a distinct separately approved operation claim");
+  assert.equal(f.calls.includes("install"),false);
+});
+
+test("boolean approvals, changed package/IDs, and bogus loaded-plist claims fail closed",t=>{
+  const f=fixture(t);
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=true;
+  assert.equal(f.advance().action_id,"macos_runner_install");
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=f.challenge("install");
+  f.advance();
+  f.runner.approval_sha256="b".repeat(64);
+  assert.equal(f.advance().error_id,"MACOS-FIRST-BOOT-IDENTITY");
+  f.runner.approval_sha256="a".repeat(64);
+  f.state.macos_runner_action_approval=f.challenge("start");
+  f.settings.online=true;f.settings.plist=false;
+  assert.equal(f.advance().error_id,"MACOS-FIRST-BOOT-SERVICE");
+  f.state.control_repository_id="999";
+  assert.equal(f.advance().error_id,"MACOS-FIRST-BOOT-IDENTITY");
+});
+
+test("official macOS installer orders all identity and native gates before first registration",()=>{
+  const begin=cli.indexOf("function runSetup() {");
+  const end=cli.indexOf("\n  state.installation_id ||= crypto.randomUUID();",begin);
+  const mac=cli.slice(begin,end);
+  const registration=mac.indexOf("registration=ensureInstallerChatRegistration");
+  for(const gate of ["github_plugin_authorization_verified!==true","verifyMacosGeneratedRepositories({state})","prepareMacosNativeHelper({state","if(!state.codex_trust_sandbox)","if(!state.reviewer_browser_ready)","const runnerBootstrap=advanceMacosFirstBootRunner(state)","runnerBootstrap.status!==\"READY\""]){
+    assert.ok(mac.indexOf(gate)>=0&&mac.indexOf(gate)<registration,gate);
+  }
+  assert.doesNotMatch(mac,/activateTargetProject\(/);
+  assert.match(mac,/MACOS-POST-SANDBOX-UNSUPPORTED/);
+  const windows=cli.slice(end,cli.indexOf("function codexLogin()",end));
+  assert.match(windows,/scripts","install-runner\.ps1/);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(windows,/advanceMacosFirstBootRunner|macos_runner_package_approval/);
+});
+
+test("only independently approved fresh identity can await its first route without legacy adoption",()=>{
+  const start=cli.indexOf("function macosSelectedAwaitingFirstRoute(");
+  const end=cli.indexOf("function macosTrustedRecoveryEvidence(",start);
+  const context=vm.createContext({});

+  assert.equal(context.awaiting({}, {...approved,status:"NEEDS_VERIFICATION"}),false);

+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import path from "node:path";
+import {verifyMacosGeneratedRepositories} from "../src/lib/control-env.mjs";
+import {defaultLocalPaths,renderWorkflow,renderConfigPs1} from "../src/lib/control-render.mjs";
+
+function githubFixture(){
+  const state={installation_id:"abcd1234-fixture",github_login:"owner",control_repository:"owner/chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-control-abcd1234",sandbox_repository:"owner/chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-sandbox-abcd1234"};
+  const records={user:{login:"owner",id:77}};
+  for(const [role,id] of [["control",101],["sandbox",202]]) records[`repos/${state[`${role}_repository`]}`]={id,full_name:state[`${role}_repository`],name:state[`${role}_repository`].split("/")[1],private:true,owner:{login:"owner",id:77},description:`ChatGPT Codex Orchestrator ${role} installation ${state.installation_id}`};
+  const calls=[];
+  const commandRunner=(command,args)=>{assert.equal(command,"gh");assert.equal(args[0],"api");calls.push(args);return {ok:true,stdout:JSON.stringify(records[args[1]])};};
+  return {state,records,calls,commandRunner};
+}
+
+test("generated Mac Control/Sandbox authority pins independently owned distinct immutable IDs",()=>{
+  const f=githubFixture();
+  assert.deepEqual(verifyMacosGeneratedRepositories(f),{control_repository_id:"101",sandbox_repository_id:"202",github_owner_id:"77"});
+  assert.equal(f.calls.length,3);
+  assert.ok(f.calls.every(args=>args[0]==="api"),"verification cannot create repositories");
+});
+
+test("missing/wrong GitHub IDs, owner, visibility, role and pinned identity are rejected",()=>{
+  for(const mutate of [
+    f=>delete f.records[`repos/${f.state.control_repository}`].id,
+    f=>f.records[`repos/${f.state.sandbox_repository}`].id=101,
+    f=>f.records[`repos/${f.state.control_repository}`].owner.id=88,
+    f=>f.records[`repos/${f.state.control_repository}`].private=false,
+    f=>f.records[`repos/${f.state.control_repository}`].description="unrelated Control",
+    f=>f.records.user.login="other",
+    f=>f.state.github_owner_id="88",
+    f=>f.state.control_repository_id="999",
+    f=>f.state.sandbox_repository=f.state.control_repository
+  ]){
+    const f=githubFixture();mutate(f);
+    assert.throws(()=>verifyMacosGeneratedRepositories(f));
+  }
+});
+
+test("first-boot paths isolate Mac runner/target and preserve Windows path conventions",()=>{
+  const args={home:"/synthetic/home",installationId:"abcd1234-fixture",projectKey:"orchestrator-dev"};
+  const mac=defaultLocalPaths({...args,platform:"darwin"});
+  const windows=defaultLocalPaths({...args,platform:"win32"});
+  const instance=path.join(args.home,".chatgpt-codex-orchestrator","instances","abcd1234");
+  assert.equal(mac.runner,path.join(instance,"runner"));
+  assert.equal(mac.project,path.join(instance,"automation",args.projectKey));
+  assert.equal(windows.runner,path.join(args.home,"ChatGPTCodexOrchestrator","runner-abcd1234"));
+  assert.equal(windows.project,path.join(args.home,"ChatGPTCodexProjects",args.projectKey));
+  assert.equal(mac.runnerLabel,windows.runnerLabel);
+  const config=renderConfigPs1({githubLogin:"owner",projectKey:"sandbox-abcd1234",projectClonePath:mac.project,runnerPath:mac.runner,browserPort:13333,browserProfile:path.join(instance,"browser-profile"),chatRouteFile:path.join(instance,"chat-routes.json"),instanceId:"runner-abcd1234"});
+  assert.match(config,/ORCHESTRATOR_INSTANCE_ID = 'runner-abcd1234'/);
+  assert.equal(renderWorkflow("runs-on: [self-hosted, {{RUNNER_OS}}, {{RUNNER_ARCH}}, {{RUNNER_LABEL}}]",{platform:"darwin",arch:"arm64",runnerLabel:mac.runnerLabel}),"runs-on: [self-hosted, macOS, ARM64, codex-orchestrator-abcd1234]");
+});

   125	  return {checked:r.ok,found,own_loaded:ownLoaded,unknown:!r.ok||(!label&&known),fail_closed:!r.ok||(!label&&known),output:r.output};

    13	export function normalizeChatUrl(value){

    18	    return u.origin+u.pathname.replace(/\/$/,"");
    19	  }catch{
    20	    throw new Error("Invalid ChatGPT conversation URL");
    21	  }

    82	function registrationMarker(state,projectKey,chatUrl){

    89	function listRegistrationIssues(state,projectKey,chatUrl,commandRunner=runGh){
    90	  const marker=registrationMarker(state,projectKey,chatUrl);
    91	  const r=commandRunner([
    92	    "issue","list","--repo",state.control_repository,

   107	export function ensureInstallerChatRegistration(state,{projectKey,commandRunner=runGh}){
   108	  if(!state?.control_repository) throw new Error("Control repository is not configured");
   109	  if(!projectKey) throw new Error("projectKey is required");
   110	  const chatUrl=normalizeChatUrl(state?.reviewer_chat_url);
   111	
   112	  const registered=findRegisteredChatRoute(state,{projectKey,chatUrl});

   115	  }

   117	  const marker=registrationMarker(state,projectKey,chatUrl);
   118	  const candidates=listRegistrationIssues(state,projectKey,chatUrl,commandRunner).filter(x=>{
   119	    if(state?.instance_platform!=="darwin") return String(x.title||"").includes(marker);
   120	    if(String(x.state||"").toUpperCase()!=="OPEN"||String(x.title||"")!=="[CHAT-REGISTER] "+marker) return false;
   121	    try{const body=JSON.parse(x.body);return body.project===projectKey&&normalizeChatUrl(body.chat_url)===chatUrl&&body.installation_id===state.installation_id;}catch{return false;}
   122	  });
   123	  if(state?.instance_platform==="darwin"&&candidates.length>1) throw new Error("Multiple registration issues match this exact Chat; refusing ambiguous submission");

   125	  if(!issue){
   126	    const title="[CHAT-REGISTER] "+marker;

   128	      project:projectKey,
   129	      chat_url:chatUrl,
   130	      installation_id:state.installation_id
   131	    },null,2);

   134	    issue={title,url};
   135	  }
   136	
   137	  return {status:"WAITING",route:null,issue};
   138	}

    97	  if(fs.existsSync(path.join(target,".git"))){
    98	    const origin=run("git",["remote","get-url","origin"],{cwd:target}).stdout;
    99	    if(process.platform==="darwin"?normalizedGithubRepository(origin)!==repository.toLowerCase():!origin.toLowerCase().includes(repository.toLowerCase())) throw new Error("Existing local folder points to a different Git repository");
   100	    run("git",["fetch","origin","--prune"],{cwd:target});
   101	    return;
   102	  }
   103	  if(fs.existsSync(target)&&fs.readdirSync(target).length>0) throw new Error("Target clone directory already exists and is not empty");
   104	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true});
   105	  run("gh",["repo","clone",repository,target]);
   106	}
   107	
   108	function inspectExistingClone(target, purpose){
   109	  if(!fs.existsSync(target)) return;
   110	  const st=fs.lstatSync(target);
   111	  if(st.isSymbolicLink() || !st.isDirectory() || (typeof process.getuid === "function" && st.uid !== process.getuid())) throw new Error(`${purpose} clone directory is symlinked, foreign-owned, or not a directory`);
   112	  const git=path.join(target,".git");
   113	  if(fs.existsSync(git)) {
   114	    const gs=fs.lstatSync(git);
   115	    if(gs.isSymbolicLink() || (typeof process.getuid === "function" && gs.uid !== process.getuid())) throw new Error(`${purpose} clone Git directory is symlinked or foreign-owned`);
   116	    const status=run("git",["status","--porcelain","--untracked-files=all"],{cwd:target}).stdout;
   117	    if(status) throw new Error(`${purpose} clone contains dirty or untracked state; refusing to fetch or overwrite it`);
   118	  } else if(fs.readdirSync(target).length) throw new Error(`${purpose} clone directory is not an empty Git checkout`);
   119	}
   120	
   121	function requireCleanOwnedClone(target, purpose){
   122	  if(!fs.existsSync(path.join(target,".git"))) return;
   123	  const status=run("git",["status","--porcelain","--untracked-files=all"],{cwd:target}).stdout;
   124	  if(status) throw new Error(`${purpose} clone contains dirty or untracked state; refusing to overwrite it`);
   125	}

   127	function commitAndPush({repoPath,message,branch="main"}){
   128	  run("git",["add","-A"],{cwd:repoPath});
   129	  const diff=run("git",["diff","--cached","--quiet"],{cwd:repoPath,allowFailure:true});
   130	  if(diff.ok) return {changed:false};
   131	  run("git",[
   132	    "-c","user.name=ChatGPT Codex Orchestrator",
   133	    "-c","user.email=codex-orchestrator@users.noreply.github.com",
   134	    "commit","-m",message
   135	  ],{cwd:repoPath});
   136	  run("git",["push","-u","origin","HEAD:"+branch],{cwd:repoPath});
   137	  return {changed:true};
   138	}

   140	function seedSandbox({repoPath,installationId}){
   141	  const marker=path.join(repoPath,".orchestrator-sandbox");
   142	  if(fs.existsSync(marker)){
   143	    const value=fs.readFileSync(marker,"utf8").trim();
   144	    if(value!==installationId) throw new Error("Existing sandbox marker belongs to a different installation");
   145	    return {changed:false};
   146	  }
   147	
   148	  if(fs.readdirSync(repoPath).filter(x=>x!==".git").length>0){
   149	    throw new Error("Generated sandbox repository is not empty and has no matching installation marker");
   150	  }
   151	
   152	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(repoPath,"src"),{recursive:true});
   153	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(repoPath,"test"),{recursive:true});
   154	  fs.writeFileSync(marker,installationId+"\n");
   155	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(repoPath,"README.md"),[
   156	    "# ChatGPT Codex Orchestrator Sandbox",
   157	    "",
   158	    "This private repository exists only for installation and end-to-end safety tests.",
   159	    "It is not a production application repository.",
   160	    ""
   161	  ].join("\n"));
   162	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(repoPath,"package.json"),JSON.stringify({
   163	    name:"chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-sandbox",
   164	    version:"1.0.0",
   165	    private:true,
   166	    type:"module",
   167	    engines:{node:">=22"},
   168	    scripts:{test:"node --test"}
   169	  },null,2)+"\n");
   170	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(repoPath,"src","status.mjs"),'export const status = "not-ready";\n');
   171	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(repoPath,"test","status.test.mjs"),[
   172	    'import test from "node:test";',
   173	    'import assert from "node:assert/strict";',
   174	    'import {status} from "../src/status.mjs";',
   175	    "",

   177	    '  assert.equal(status,"not-ready");',
   178	    "});",
   179	    ""
   180	  ].join("\n"));
   181	
   182	  return commitAndPush({
   183	    repoPath,
   184	    message:"chore: initialize isolated orchestrator sandbox",
   185	    branch:"main"
   186	  });
   187	}

   197	export function activateTargetProject({controlClonePath,projectKey,repository}){
   198	  if(!controlClonePath||!projectKey||!repository) throw new Error("Target activation requires control clone, project key and repository");
   199	  const registryPath=path.join(controlClonePath,"projects.json");
   200	  if(!fs.existsSync(registryPath)) throw new Error("Control project registry is missing");
   201	
   202	  const registry=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(registryPath,"utf8"));
   203	  const project=registry?.projects?.[projectKey];
   204	  if(!project) throw new Error("Target project is missing from the control registry");
   205	  if(project.repository!==repository) throw new Error("Target activation repository does not match registry");
   206	
   207	  if(project.enabled===true) return {changed:false,already_enabled:true};
   208	
   209	  project.enabled=true;
   210	  fs.writeFileSync(registryPath,JSON.stringify(registry,null,2)+"\n");
   211	  const commit=commitAndPush({
   212	    repoPath:controlClonePath,
   213	    message:"chore: activate verified application project",
   214	    branch:"main"
   215	  });
   216	  return {changed:commit.changed,already_enabled:false};
   217	}

   226	function stopRetiredOriginRouter(controlPath){
   227	  if(process.platform!=="win32") return {stopped:0};
   228	  const needle=path.join(controlPath,"scripts","origin-router-loop.ps1").toLowerCase();
   229	  const ps=[
   230	    "$needle=$env:ORCH_RETIRED_ROUTER_NEEDLE",
   231	    "$matches=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -and $_.CommandLine.ToLowerInvariant().Contains($needle) })",
   232	    "$count=0",
   233	    "foreach($p in $matches){ try { Stop-Process -Id $p.ProcessId -Force -ErrorAction Stop; $count++ } catch {} }",
   234	    "Write-Output $count"
   235	  ].join("; ");
   236	  const r=spawnSafeSync("powershell.exe",["-NoProfile","-Command",ps],{
   237	    encoding:"utf8",
   238	    env:{...process.env,ORCH_RETIRED_ROUTER_NEEDLE:needle}
   239	  });
   240	  return {stopped:r.status===0?Number((r.stdout||"0").trim())||0:0};
   241	}

   250	  return 10000+(Number.isFinite(n)?n%40000:0);
   251	}

    53	export function assertSafeRuntimePath(file, { allowMissing = false, type } = {}) {
    54	  if (typeof file !== "string" || !path.isAbsolute(file)) throw new Error("Runtime path must be absolute");
    55	  if (type !== undefined && !["file", "directory"].includes(type)) throw new Error("Invalid runtime path type");
    56	  const absolute = path.resolve(file);
    57	  if (file !== absolute) throw new Error("Runtime path must be canonical without dot segments or aliases");
    58	  let current = path.parse(absolute).root;
    59	  const parts = absolute.slice(current.length).split(path.sep).filter(Boolean);
    60	  for (let i = -1; i < parts.length; i++) {
    61	    if (i >= 0) current = path.join(current, parts[i]);
    62	    let st;
    63	    try { st = fs.lstatSync(current); }
    64	    catch (error) {
    65	      if (allowMissing && error.code === "ENOENT") return absolute;
    66	      throw error;
    67	    }
    68	    if (st.isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error(`Symlinked runtime path is forbidden: ${current}`);
    69	    const leaf = i === parts.length - 1;
    70	    if (!leaf && !st.isDirectory()) throw new Error(`Runtime path ancestor is not a directory: ${current}`);

    73	      || (!st.isFile() && !st.isDirectory()))) throw new Error(`Runtime path has the wrong type: ${current}`);
    74	  }
    75	  return absolute;
    76	}

    81	}

    83	function preparedFiles(files) {
    84	  if (!Array.isArray(files) || files.length !== MANIFEST.length) throw new Error("Runtime payload is incomplete");
    85	  const seen = new Set();

+  process.env.HOME=home;
+  const instance=path.join(home,"instance"),runner=path.join(instance,"runner"),executable=path.join(runner,"bin","Runner.Listener"),helper=path.join(instance,"runtime","native","macos","process-identity");
+  for(const dir of [path.dirname(executable),path.dirname(helper)]) fs.mkdirSync(dir,{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
+  fs.writeFileSync(executable,"synthetic executable never run\n",{mode:0o700});fs.writeFileSync(helper,"synthetic helper never run\n",{mode:0o700});
+  const state={sandbox_only:true,target_environment_ready:false,instance_id:"instance-1",installation_id:"install-1",runner_owner:"install-1",runner_label:"runner-1",launch_agent_label:"com.chatgpt.codex.runner",runner_path:runner,instance_root:instance,macos_setup_approved:true,macos_action_approval:"install"};
+  const capability={status:"PASS",installation_id:state.installation_id,instance_path:instance,helper_path:helper,receipt_path:path.join(instance,"macos-native-helper-capability.json")};
+  const runnerIdentity={status:"PASS",runner_executable:executable,program_arguments:["run"]};
+  const calls=[];let loaded=false;
+  const commandRunner=(command,args)=>{calls.push({command,args});if(command!=="launchctl")throw new Error("No real command is permitted by this fixture");if(args[0]==="list")return {ok:true,output:loaded?`123 0 ${state.launch_agent_label}`:""};if(args[0]==="bootstrap"){loaded=true;return {ok:true,output:""};}if(args[0]==="kickstart")return {ok:true,output:""};if(args[0]==="print")return {ok:loaded,output:loaded?"pid = 123":"not-loaded"};return {ok:false,output:"unexpected"};};
+  const options={state,home,platform:"darwin",arch:"arm64",commandRunner,runnerInspector:()=>runnerIdentity,capabilityVerifier:()=>capability};
+  return {home,instance,runner,executable,helper,state,capability,calls,options,cleanup(){if(previousHome===undefined)delete process.env.HOME;else process.env.HOME=previousHome;fs.rmSync(home,{recursive:true,force:true});}};
+}

+    github_plugin_authorized: true, github_plugin_authorization_verified: true, generated_repo_authorized: true

+  writeJson(path.join(instance, "migration-intent.json"), { schema_version: 1, phase: "PREPARED", installation_id: installationId, generated_repositories: { control: state.control_repository, sandbox: state.sandbox_repository }, destination: { path: instance } });
+  writeJson(state.chat_route_file, { ...identity, routes: [] });
+  const calls = [];

+    full_name: state[`${role}_repository`], private: true, visibility: "private", owner: { login: state.github_login, id: 303 },

+    assert.deepEqual(inspectSelected(f).missing_verification_gates, ["independent_generated_repository_identity"]);
+    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(path.join(f.instance, "selected-fresh-instance-approval-v1.json")), false);
+  }
+});
+
+test("selected approval pins repository IDs across resume instead of trusting a receipt label", t => {
+  const f = selectedFixture(t);
+  const approved = approveSelected(f);
+  const before = fs.readFileSync(approved.approval_path);
+  f.repositories.get(f.state.control_repository).id = 999;
+  assert.deepEqual(inspectSelected(f).missing_verification_gates, ["selected_approval_identity"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(approved.approval_path), before);
+});
+
+test("selected mode rejects unsafe ancestors, instance permissions, links, and mismatched identities", t => {
+  for (const kind of ["ancestor", "directory-mode", "symlink", "hardlink", "identity", "route-identity", "missing-intent", "missing-routes", "wrong-os", "wrong-arch", "origin", "repository-id"]) {
+    const f = selectedFixture(t);
+    if (kind === "ancestor") fs.chmodSync(path.join(f.home, ".chatgpt-codex-orchestrator"), 0o777);
+    if (kind === "directory-mode") fs.chmodSync(f.instance, 0o755);
+    if (kind === "identity") writeJson(path.join(f.instance, "instance.json"), { ...f.identity, installation_id: "foreign-installation" });
+    if (kind === "route-identity") writeJson(f.state.chat_route_file, { ...f.identity, control_repository: "foreign/control", routes: [] });
+    if (kind === "missing-intent") fs.rmSync(path.join(f.instance, "migration-intent.json"));
+    if (kind === "missing-routes") fs.rmSync(f.state.chat_route_file);
+    if (kind === "origin") writeJson(path.join(f.control, "instance.json"), { schema_version: 1, ...f.identity, role: "control", platform: "darwin", arch: "arm64", origin: `https://github.com/foreign/${f.state.control_repository}.git` });
+    if (kind === "repository-id") writeJson(path.join(f.control, "instance.json"), { schema_version: 1, ...f.identity, role: "control", platform: "darwin", arch: "arm64", control_repository_id: 999 });
+    if (kind === "wrong-os" || kind === "wrong-arch") writeJson(path.join(f.control, "instance.json"), { ...f.identity, role: "control", platform: kind === "wrong-os" ? "win32" : "darwin", arch: kind === "wrong-arch" ? "x64" : "arm64" });
+    if (kind === "symlink") {
+      const file = path.join(f.instance, "migration-intent.json");
+      const outside = path.join(f.home, "outside.json");
+      fs.renameSync(file, outside);
+      fs.symlinkSync(outside, file);
+    }
+    if (kind === "hardlink") fs.linkSync(path.join(f.instance, "migration-intent.json"), path.join(f.home, "hardlinked-intent.json"));
+    assert.equal(inspectSelected(f).status, "CONFLICT", kind);
+  }
+});
+
+test("selected mode rejects historical identity and alias collisions without modifying either root", t => {
+  for (const kind of ["installation", "repository-name", "repository-id", "alias"]) {
+    const f = selectedFixture(t);
+    const historical = path.join(f.instances, "1832f0bb");
+    if (kind === "alias") fs.symlinkSync(f.instance, historical);
+    else {
+      fs.mkdirSync(historical, { mode: 0o700 });
+      writeJson(path.join(historical, "instance.json"), kind === "installation" ? { installation_id: f.state.installation_id } : kind === "repository-name" ? { sandbox_repository: f.state.control_repository } : kind === "repository-id" ? { control_repository_id: 202 } : {});
+    }
+    assert.equal(inspectSelected(f).status, "CONFLICT", kind);
+    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(path.join(f.instance, "selected-fresh-instance-approval-v1.json")), false);
+  }
+});
+
+test("selected mode rejects case-folded instance-name collisions on every filesystem", t => {
+  const f = selectedFixture(t);
+  const fsImpl = { ...fs, readdirSync: (file, options) => file === f.instances && options === undefined ? [f.state.instance_id, f.state.instance_id.toUpperCase()] : fs.readdirSync(file, options) };
+  const result = inspectMacosSelectedFreshInstance({ state: f.state, home: f.home, githubApi: f.githubApi, fsImpl });
+  assert.deepEqual(result.missing_verification_gates, ["colliding_instance_name"]);
+});
+
+test("an already populated route registry cannot acquire fresh-instance approval", t => {
+  const f = selectedFixture(t);
+  writeJson(f.state.chat_route_file, { ...f.identity, routes: [{ route: "chat-existing", project: f.state.sandbox_project_key, status: "active", ...f.identity }] });
+  const report = inspectSelected(f);
+  assert.equal(report.status, "NEEDS_USER_ACTION");
+  assert.equal(report.fresh_approval_eligible, false);
+  assert.throws(() => approveSelected(f, report.approval_challenge), /unused fresh instance/);
+});
+
+test("approved selected instance can resume after first registration without making that route its authority", t => {
+  const f = selectedFixture(t);
+  const result = approveSelected(f);
+  const before = fs.readFileSync(result.approval_path);
+  writeJson(f.state.chat_route_file, { ...f.identity, routes: [{ route: "chat-new", project: f.state.sandbox_project_key, status: "active", ...f.identity }] });
+  assert.equal(inspectSelected(f).status, "SELECTED_FRESH_INSTANCE_VERIFIED");
+  assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(result.approval_path), before);
+});
+
+test("selected instance ownership is current-user authority rather than root or a foreign uid", t => {
+  if (typeof process.getuid !== "function") return t.skip("uid ownership is unavailable");
+  const f = selectedFixture(t);
+  const fsImpl = { ...fs, lstatSync: file => {
+    const stat = fs.lstatSync(file);
+    if (file === f.instance) Object.defineProperty(stat, "uid", { value: process.getuid() + 1 });
+    return stat;
+  } };
+  const result = inspectMacosSelectedFreshInstance({ state: f.state, home: f.home, githubApi: f.githubApi, fsImpl });
+  assert.deepEqual(result.missing_verification_gates, ["private_owned_selected_instance"]);
+  assert.equal(f.calls.length, 0);
+});
+
+test("selected-only passive Runner work accepts owned checkout modes and still refuses hardlinks", t => {
+  const f = selectedFixture(t);
+  approveSelected(f);
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(f.instance, "runner"), { mode: 0o700 });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(f.instance, "runner", "_work"), { mode: 0o755 });
+  const checkout = path.join(f.instance, "runner", "_work", "README.md");
+  fs.writeFileSync(checkout, "synthetic checked-out bytes\n", { mode: 0o644 });
+  assert.equal(inspectSelected(f).status, "SELECTED_FRESH_INSTANCE_VERIFIED");
+  // The global legacy scan retains its stricter existing policy.
+  assert.equal(inspectMacosExistingInstanceRecovery({ home: f.home }).status, "CONFLICT");
+  fs.linkSync(checkout, path.join(f.home, "outside-checkout-hardlink"));
+  assert.deepEqual(inspectSelected(f).missing_verification_gates, ["safe_selected_instance_tree"]);
+});
+

+  assert.throws(() => verifyMacosGeneratedRepositoryIdentity({ state: { ...state, github_plugin_authorization_installation_id: "foreign" }, githubApi: api }), /exact first-install authorization/);
+  assert.equal(calls.length, 3);
+});

+  assert.deepEqual(api.getAuthenticatedUser(), { login: "synthetic", id: 42 });

+    ["api", "--hostname", "github.com", "repos/synthetic/control"],
+    ["api", "--hostname", "github.com", "repos/synthetic/control/issues/410"]
+  ]);
+});

+  for(const mutate of [e=>delete e.repository.id,e=>e.repository.id=9002,e=>e.repository.full_name="owner/foreign",e=>e.issue.body=JSON.stringify({project:"alpha",chat_url:"https://chatgpt.com/c/chat-165"}),e=>e.issue.state="closed"]){
+    const f=macFixture(),file=f.event(),event=readJson(file);mutate(event);privateJson(file,event);assert.throws(()=>f.call(file,()=>assert.fail("unauthorized delivery")),/identity/);assert.equal(fs.existsSync(f.outbox()),false);
+  }
+  for(const env of [{RUNNER_OS:"Windows"},{RUNNER_ARCH:"X64"},{RUNNER_NAME:"other-project-runner"}]){const f=macFixture();assert.throws(()=>f.call(f.event(),()=>assert.fail("wrong Runner"),true,env),/macOS ARM64/);}
+});
+test("obsolete closed manual #409 cannot replay and a brand-new Chat registers separately",()=>{
+  const f=macFixture(),file=f.event("orchestrator-dev",409,"https://chatgpt.com/c/manual-408"),event=readJson(file);event.issue.state="closed";event.issue.body=JSON.stringify({project:"orchestrator-dev",chat_url:"https://chatgpt.com/c/manual-408"});privateJson(file,event);
+  assert.throws(()=>f.call(file,()=>assert.fail("manual #409 must never deliver")),/Obsolete manual registration #409/);
+  f.call(f.event(),userReceipt);f.call(f.event("alpha",166,"https://chatgpt.com/c/new-chat"),userReceipt);
+  const routes=readJson(f.metadata.chat_route_file).routes;assert.deepEqual(routes.map(x=>x.route),["chat-165","chat-166"]);assert.equal(routes[1].chat_url,"https://chatgpt.com/c/new-chat");
+});
+test("selected Mac callback writes leave historical instance bytes unchanged and reject aliases",()=>{
+  const f=macFixture(),old=path.join(path.dirname(f.base),"1832f0bb");fs.mkdirSync(old,{mode:0o755});const legacy=path.join(old,"unsafe-legacy.json");fs.writeFileSync(legacy,"historical bytes",{mode:0o666});const before=fs.readFileSync(legacy);const mode=fs.statSync(legacy).mode;
+  f.call(f.event(),userReceipt);assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(legacy),before);assert.equal(fs.statSync(legacy).mode,mode);
+  fs.rmSync(f.outbox());fs.linkSync(legacy,f.outbox());assert.throws(()=>f.call(f.event(),()=>assert.fail("hardlink must not be delivered")),/Untrusted registration path/);assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(legacy),before);
+});
+test("installer lookup failures and ambiguous issues cannot trigger duplicate registration submissions",()=>{
+  const f=macFixture(),state={...f.metadata,instance_platform:"darwin",reviewer_chat_url:"https://chatgpt.com/c/chat-165"};
+  for(const result of [{ok:false,stdout:"",stderr:"offline"},{ok:true,stdout:"bad-json"},{ok:true,stdout:"{}"},{ok:true,stdout:""}]){
+    let creates=0;assert.throws(()=>ensureInstallerChatRegistration(state,{projectKey:"alpha",commandRunner:args=>{if(args[1]==="create")creates++;return result;}}),/lookup/);assert.equal(creates,0);
+  }
+  let creates=0;
+  assert.throws(()=>ensureInstallerChatRegistration(state,{projectKey:"alpha",commandRunner:args=>{
+    if(args[1]==="create"){creates++;return {ok:true,stdout:"https://github.com/owner/control-new/issues/1"};}
+    const marker=args[args.indexOf("--search")+1].replace(/ in:title$/,"");
+    return {ok:true,stdout:JSON.stringify([1,2].map(number=>({number,title:`[CHAT-REGISTER] ${marker}`,state:"OPEN",body:JSON.stringify({project:"alpha",chat_url:state.reviewer_chat_url,installation_id:state.installation_id})})))};
+  }}),/Multiple registration issues/);assert.equal(creates,0);
+});
+test("installer does not reuse closed #409 or an issue belonging to a different Chat",()=>{
+  const f=macFixture(),state={...f.metadata,instance_platform:"darwin",reviewer_chat_url:"https://chatgpt.com/c/chat-165"};const created=[];
+  const commandRunner=args=>{
+    const title=args[args.indexOf("--title")+1];
+    if(args[1]==="create"){created.push(title);return {ok:true,stdout:`https://github.com/owner/control-new/issues/${500+created.length}`};}
+    const marker=args[args.indexOf("--search")+1].replace(/ in:title$/,"");
+    return {ok:true,stdout:JSON.stringify([{number:409,title:`[CHAT-REGISTER] ${marker}`,state:"CLOSED",body:JSON.stringify({project:"alpha",chat_url:state.reviewer_chat_url,installation_id:state.installation_id})},{number:410,title:`[CHAT-REGISTER] ${marker}`,state:"OPEN",body:JSON.stringify({project:"alpha",chat_url:"https://chatgpt.com/c/old-chat",installation_id:state.installation_id})}])};
+  };
+  assert.equal(ensureInstallerChatRegistration(state,{projectKey:"alpha",commandRunner}).status,"WAITING");
+  assert.equal(ensureInstallerChatRegistration({...state,reviewer_chat_url:"https://chatgpt.com/c/new-chat"},{projectKey:"alpha",commandRunner}).status,"WAITING");
+  assert.equal(created.length,2);assert.notEqual(created[0],created[1]);
+});
+test("installer READY rejects duplicate route bindings, wrong issue pairs and unsafe private ancestors",()=>{
+  const f=macFixture(),event=f.event();f.call(event,userReceipt);
+  const state={...f.metadata,instance_platform:"darwin"},options={projectKey:"alpha",chatUrl:"https://chatgpt.com/c/chat-165"};const registry=readJson(f.metadata.chat_route_file);
+  privateJson(f.metadata.chat_route_file,{version:1,routes:[...registry.routes,...registry.routes]});assert.equal(findRegisteredChatRoute(state,options),null);
+  privateJson(f.metadata.chat_route_file,{version:1,routes:[{...registry.routes[0],registration_issue:166}]});assert.equal(findRegisteredChatRoute(state,options),null);
+  privateJson(f.metadata.chat_route_file,registry);fs.chmodSync(path.dirname(f.base),0o755);assert.equal(findRegisteredChatRoute(state,options),null);
+});

+import test, { afterEach } from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import fs from "node:fs";
+import os from "node:os";
+import path from "node:path";
+import crypto from "node:crypto";
+import { gzipSync } from "node:zlib";
+import { inspectMacosRunnerBootstrap, approveMacosRunnerBootstrap, MACOS_RUNNER_RUNTIME_PATH } from "../src/lib/macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs";
+
+const hash=bytes=>crypto.createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex");
+const syntheticHomes=[];
+afterEach(()=>{for(const home of syntheticHomes.splice(0))fs.rmSync(home,{recursive:true,force:true});});
+function syntheticArchive(binary) {
+  const header=Buffer.alloc(512);header.write("bin/Runner.Listener");header.write("0000700\0",100);header.write("0000000\0",108);header.write("0000000\0",116);header.write(binary.length.toString(8).padStart(11,"0")+"\0",124);header.write("00000000000\0",136);header.fill(32,148,156);header[156]=48;header.write("ustar\0",257);
+  const checksum=header.reduce((sum,byte)=>sum+byte,0);header.write(checksum.toString(8).padStart(6,"0")+"\0 ",148);
+  return gzipSync(Buffer.concat([header,binary,Buffer.alloc((512-binary.length%512)%512),Buffer.alloc(1024)]));
+}
+function fixture() {
+  const home=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir()),"orch-first-runner-")),short="abcdef12",root=path.join(home,".chatgpt-codex-orchestrator","instances",short),runner=path.join(root,"runner"),executable=path.join(runner,"bin","Runner.Listener"),control=path.join(home,"control"),codex=path.join(home,"codex");
+  syntheticHomes.push(home);
+  const state={installation_id:"abcdef12-3456",instance_id:short,instance_root:root,runner_path:runner,runner_owner:"abcdef12-3456",runner_label:`codex-orchestrator-${short}`,launch_agent_label:`com.chatgpt.codex.${short}`,sandbox_only:true,target_environment_ready:false,github_login:"owner",control_repository:`owner/chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-control-${short}`,control_repository_id:"101",sandbox_repository:`owner/chatgpt-codex-orchestrator-sandbox-${short}`,sandbox_repository_id:"202",sandbox_project_key:`sandbox-${short}`,browser_profile:path.join(root,"browser-profile"),browser_port:13333,pending_wake_outbox:path.join(root,"pending-wakes"),control_clone_path:control};
+  for(const dir of [path.dirname(executable),state.browser_profile,state.pending_wake_outbox,control])fs.mkdirSync(dir,{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
+  const binary=Buffer.alloc(32);binary.writeUInt32LE(0xfeedfacf,0);binary.writeUInt32LE(0x0100000c,4);binary.writeUInt32LE(2,12);
+  fs.writeFileSync(executable,binary,{mode:0o700});fs.writeFileSync(codex,"synthetic Codex never executed\n",{mode:0o700});fs.writeFileSync(path.join(home,"gh"),"synthetic gh never executed\n",{mode:0o700});
+  for(const name of [".credentials",".credentials_rsaparams"])fs.writeFileSync(path.join(runner,name),"{}",{mode:0o600});
+  const config={agentId:303,agentName:state.runner_label,gitHubUrl:`https://github.com/${state.control_repository}`,serverUrl:"https://example.actions.githubusercontent.com/runner/",workFolder:"_work"};
+  const writeJson=(file,value)=>fs.writeFileSync(file,JSON.stringify(value),{mode:0o600});writeJson(path.join(runner,".runner"),config);
+  writeJson(path.join(control,"instance.json"),{schema_version:1,role:"control",installation_id:state.installation_id,instance_id:short,instance_root:root,platform:"darwin",arch:"arm64",runner_label:state.runner_label,launch_agent_label:state.launch_agent_label,trusted_github_username:state.github_login,control_repository:state.control_repository,sandbox_repository:state.sandbox_repository,control_repository_id:"101",sandbox_repository_id:"202",browser_profile:state.browser_profile,browser_port:state.browser_port,pending_wake_outbox:state.pending_wake_outbox,codex_executable:codex});
+  writeJson(path.join(control,"projects.json"),{projects:{[state.sandbox_project_key]:{repository:state.sandbox_repository,enabled:true},"orchestrator-dev":{repository:"owner/orchestrator-dev",enabled:false}}});
+  const archive=syntheticArchive(binary);fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root,"runner-package.tar.gz"),archive,{mode:0o600});
+  const approval={schema_version:1,installation_id:state.installation_id,instance_id:short,instance_path:root,project:state.sandbox_project_key,control_repository:state.control_repository,sandbox_repository:state.sandbox_repository,control_repository_id:"101",sandbox_repository_id:"202",runner_path:runner,runner_executable:executable,runner_label:state.runner_label,launch_agent_label:state.launch_agent_label,browser_profile:state.browser_profile,browser_port:state.browser_port,pending_wake_outbox:state.pending_wake_outbox,source_version:"2.999.0",source_url:"https://github.com/actions/runner/releases/download/v2.999.0/actions-runner-osx-arm64-2.999.0.tar.gz",archive_sha256:hash(archive),executable_sha256:hash(binary),binary_manifest:{"bin/Runner.Listener":hash(binary)},config_sha256:hash(fs.readFileSync(path.join(runner,".runner"))),runner_id:"303",github_user_id:"404",approved_action:"use-existing-official-macos-arm64-runner",approved_by:"owner",approval_id:"human-fixture123",approved_at:"2026-10-22T00:00:00.000Z"};
+  approval.source_release_id="505";approval.source_asset_id="606";approval.runtime_path=MACOS_RUNNER_RUNTIME_PATH;
+  const receipt=path.join(root,"macos-runner-approval.json"),candidate=path.join(root,"macos-runner-candidate.json");writeJson(receipt,approval);writeJson(candidate,approval);
+  const remoteRunner={id:303,name:state.runner_label,os:"osx",status:"online",busy:false,labels:["self-hosted","macOS","ARM64",state.runner_label].map(name=>({name}))};
+  const calls=[];
+  const commandRunner=(command,args)=>{
+    calls.push({command,args});
+    if(command==="/usr/bin/which")return {ok:true,output:`/usr/local/bin/${args[0]}`};
+    if(command==="/usr/sbin/lsof")return {ok:true,output:`p123\ncGoogle Chrome\nn127.0.0.1:${state.browser_port}\n`};
+    if(command==="/bin/ps")return args.includes("-axo")?{ok:true,output:`${process.getuid?.()||0} 456 ${executable} run`}:{ok:true,output:`${process.getuid?.()||0} /Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome --user-data-dir=${state.browser_profile} --remote-debugging-port=${state.browser_port}`};
+    if(command===codex)return {ok:true,output:"Logged in using ChatGPT"};
+    if(command!=="gh")throw new Error("Unexpected synthetic command");
+    const endpoint=args.at(-1);
+    if(endpoint==="user")return {ok:true,output:JSON.stringify({id:404,login:"owner"})};
+    if(endpoint.startsWith("repos/actions/runner/releases/"))return {ok:true,output:JSON.stringify({id:505,tag_name:"v2.999.0",draft:false,prerelease:false,assets:[{id:606,name:"actions-runner-osx-arm64-2.999.0.tar.gz",browser_download_url:approval.source_url,digest:`sha256:${approval.archive_sha256}`,state:"uploaded"}]})};
+    if(endpoint.includes("/actions/runners"))return {ok:true,output:JSON.stringify({total_count:1,runners:[remoteRunner]})};
+    const sandbox=endpoint===`repos/${state.sandbox_repository}`;return {ok:true,output:JSON.stringify({id:sandbox?202:101,full_name:sandbox?state.sandbox_repository:state.control_repository,private:true,owner:{id:404,login:"owner"}})};
+  };
+  const fsImpl=Object.create(fs);
+  fsImpl.lstatSync=file=>MACOS_RUNNER_RUNTIME_PATH.split(":").includes(file)?fs.lstatSync(home):fs.lstatSync(file);
+  fsImpl.realpathSync=file=>file==="/usr/local/bin/node"?fs.realpathSync(process.execPath):file==="/usr/local/bin/gh"?path.join(home,"gh"):fs.realpathSync(file);
+  return {home,root,runner,executable,codex,state,approval,receipt,candidate,remoteRunner,calls,writeJson,options:{state,home,platform:"darwin",arch:"arm64",commandRunner,fsImpl}};
+}
+
+test("strict bootstrap independently verifies live Mac ARM64 runner without mutations",()=>{
+  const f=fixture(),before=fs.readFileSync(f.receipt);const result=inspectMacosRunnerBootstrap({...f.options,requireOnline:true});
+  assert.equal(result.status,"PASS");assert.equal(result.runner_executable,f.executable);assert.deepEqual(result.program_arguments,["run"]);assert.equal(result.runner_id,"303");assert.equal(result.approval_sha256,hash(before));assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(f.receipt),before);assert.equal(f.calls.some(x=>["launchctl","config.sh","curl","tar"].includes(x.command)),false);
+});
+
+test("historical unsafe instance bytes are never examined or changed by selected runner inspection",()=>{
+  const f=fixture(),old=path.join(path.dirname(f.root),"1832f0bb");fs.mkdirSync(old,{mode:0o755});const file=path.join(old,"legacy.json"),bytes=Buffer.from("unsafe historical root preserved byte for byte\n");fs.writeFileSync(file,bytes,{mode:0o666});const before=fs.lstatSync(file);
+  assert.equal(inspectMacosRunnerBootstrap(f.options).status,"PASS");assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(file),bytes);assert.equal(fs.lstatSync(file).mode,before.mode);assert.equal(fs.lstatSync(file).ino,before.ino);
+});
+
+test("wrong missing and colliding immutable repository identities fail before launch",()=>{
+  for(const patch of [{control_repository_id:undefined},{sandbox_repository_id:"101"},{control_repository_id:"999"}]){

+  }

+

+  const f=fixture();f.remoteRunner.status="offline";assert.equal(inspectMacosRunnerBootstrap({...f.options,requireOnline:true}).error_id,"MACOS-RUNNER-OFFLINE");assert.equal(inspectMacosRunnerBootstrap(f.options).status,"PASS");
+  const extra=(command,args)=>command==="gh"&&args.at(-1).includes("/actions/runners")?{ok:true,output:JSON.stringify({total_count:2,runners:[f.remoteRunner,{...f.remoteRunner,id:999}]})}:f.options.commandRunner(command,args);

+test("extra local runner and unsafe selected HOME ancestors are refused without mutation",()=>{

  1166	    current_step:state.current_step||"not_started",
  1167	    completed:state.completed||[],
  1168	    project_key:state.project_key||null,
  1169	    runtime:runtimeChecks(state)
  1170	  });
  1171	}
  1172	
  1173	function repair() {
  1174	  const state=loadState();
  1175	  const lang=ensureStateLanguage(state);
  1176	  if(process.platform==="darwin") return emit({status:"NEEDS_USER_ACTION",preferred_language:lang,message:"macOS repair is approval-gated; no service or runner was changed.",migration:migrationPreflight({state}),mutations_performed:false});
  1177	  if(process.platform!=="win32"||!state.control_environment_ready||!state.control_clone_path||!state.project_key){
  1178	    return emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"REPAIR-001",recoverable:true,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"repair.not_ready"),runtime:runtimeChecks(state)},1);
  1179	  }
  1180	
  1181	  const script=path.join(state.control_clone_path,"scripts","start-orchestrator-session.ps1");
  1182	  const r=spawnSafeSync("powershell.exe",["-NoProfile","-ExecutionPolicy","Bypass","-File",script,"-Project",state.project_key],{encoding:"utf8"});
  1183	  if(r.status!==0){
  1184	    state.last_error={error_id:"REPAIR-001",message:(r.stderr||r.stdout||"Repair failed").trim(),at:new Date().toISOString()};
  1185	    saveState(state);
  1186	    return emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"REPAIR-001",recoverable:true,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"repair.failed"),details:state.last_error.message,runtime:runtimeChecks(state)},1);
  1187	  }
  1188	
  1189	  const runtime=runtimeChecks(state);
  1190	  const health=repairHealth(runtime,{preflightEstablished:true});
  1191	  if(health.status!=="PASS"){
  1192	    return emit({status:health.status,error_id:health.error_id,recoverable:health.recoverable,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,health.reason_key),repaired:health.repaired,runtime},1);
  1193	  }
  1194	  emit({status:"PASS",preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"repair.success"),repaired:health.repaired,runtime});
  1195	}
  1196	
  1197	function refreshRuntime() {
  1198	  const state=loadState();
  1199	  const lang=ensureStateLanguage(state);
  1200	  try {
  1201	    const result=refreshControlRuntime({state,sourceRoot:process.cwd()});
  1202	    state.runtime_refreshed_at=new Date().toISOString();
  1203	    if(result.changed) state.control_commit=spawnSafeSync("git",["rev-parse","HEAD"],{cwd:state.control_clone_path,encoding:"utf8"}).stdout.trim();
  1204	    saveState(state);
  1205	    emit({status:"PASS",preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"runtime.refresh.success"),...result});
  1206	  } catch(error) {
  1207	    emit({status:"ERROR",error_id:"RUNTIME-REFRESH-001",recoverable:true,preferred_language:lang,message:t(lang,"runtime.refresh.failed"),details:String(error.message||error)},1);
  1208	  }
  1209	}
  1210	
  1211	function recoveryPreflight() {
  1212	  if (process.platform !== "darwin") return emit({status:"PASS",preferred_language:"en",mutations_performed:false,existing_instance_recovery:{status:"UNSUPPORTED",platform:process.platform}});
  1213	  let state;

codex
Three blockers are now supported by source traces: the selected-instance scan rejects the required live browser profile, package validation omits bundled action executables, and an unchecked runner `.env` can override the verified launch environment. The relevant regression tests use synthetic files and empty profiles, so they miss these cases. I’m treating external provisioning and conservative callback retry recovery as implementation incompleteness rather than additional blockers.
codex
Reviewed the full 26-file cumulative diff from `9850eb75642a872b3d21f5ed329ea10d5cdd927e` to `b18933a5f1c32d2d8e9c9342d45d9a1c0f3aab6b`. **Three blocking findings remain:**

1. **P1 — Required browser login blocks selected-instance resume.**  
   [macos-existing-instance-recovery.mjs:360](/Users/Jackie/Developer/ChatGPT-Codex-Orchestrator-M2-Bridge-20261022/src/lib/macos-existing-instance-recovery.mjs:360) recursively scans the selected root, rejecting every symlink at line 93—including browser-profile entries. Chromium creates singleton symlinks in that profile during normal operation. Meanwhile, [macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs:187](/Users/Jackie/Developer/ChatGPT-Codex-Orchestrator-M2-Bridge-20261022/src/lib/macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs:187) requires that exact profile and a live browser. Completing browser login therefore makes the next selected resume fail before runner advancement; closing the browser fails the live-listener requirement. [Chromium implementation](https://chromium.googlesource.com/chromium/src/+/HEAD/chrome/browser/process_singleton_posix.cc)

2. **P1 — Package approval omits executables used by workflow actions.**  
   [macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs:160](/Users/Jackie/Developer/ChatGPT-Codex-Orchestrator-M2-Bridge-20261022/src/lib/macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs:160) includes only `bin/` archive members, and extracted-file verification likewise checks only `bin/`. Missing or modified `runner/externals/node20/bin/node` leaves inspection and approval unchanged. The generated workflow immediately invokes `actions/checkout@v4`, whose Node interpreter comes from `externals`. Consequently, an approved runner can fail checkout or execute an unapproved replacement interpreter with workflow authority. [Runner action handler](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/actions/runner/v2.329.0/src/Runner.Worker/Handlers/NodeScriptActionHandler.cs), [checkout runtime declaration](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/actions/checkout/v4/action.yml)

3. **P1 — Unchecked `.env` overrides the verified launch environment.**  
   [macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs:203](/Users/Jackie/Developer/ChatGPT-Codex-Orchestrator-M2-Bridge-20261022/src/lib/macos-runner-bootstrap.mjs:203) verifies binaries and registration files without inspecting or binding `runner/.env`. Official `Runner.Listener` loads that file directly and overwrites inherited environment variables. A private `.env` containing `PATH=/foreign/bin` therefore passes inspection but redirects the workflow’s plain `node` command, defeating the PATH pinned by [macos-platform.mjs:146](/Users/Jackie/Developer/ChatGPT-Codex-Orchestrator-M2-Bridge-20261022/src/lib/macos-platform.mjs:146). This requires neither a race nor modification after approval. [Listener implementation](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/actions/runner/v2.329.0/src/Runner.Listener/Program.cs)

Regression coverage misses these cases: browser fixtures are empty, runner archives contain only a synthetic Listener, and there are no `externals` or `.env` rejection cases.

External runner acquisition/registration remains explicitly documented implementation incompleteness. Conservative callback retry recovery is also incomplete; I did not count it or hypothetical collision scenarios as additional blockers. No separate deterministic blocker was found in exact user-role receipt matching or Windows invariants.

Read-only review only. No files or refs changed, tests/builds run, secrets accessed, or services started.
